{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Base directory for this skill: /logs/agent/sessions/skills/casadi-ipopt-nlp\n\n# CasADi + IPOPT for Nonlinear Programming\n\nCasADi is a symbolic framework for nonlinear optimization. IPOPT is an interior-point solver for large-scale NLP.\n\n## Quick start (Linux)\n\n```bash\napt-get update -qq && apt-get install -y -qq libgfortran5\npip install numpy==1.26.4 casadi==3.6.7\n```\n\n## Building an NLP\n\n### 1. Decision variables\n\n```python\nimport casadi as ca\n\nn_bus, n_gen = 100, 20\nVm = ca.MX.sym(\"Vm\", n_bus)   # Voltage magnitudes\nVa = ca.MX.sym(\"Va\", n_bus)   # Voltage angles (radians)\nPg = ca.MX.sym(\"Pg\", n_gen)   # Real power\nQg = ca.MX.sym(\"Qg\", n_gen)   # Reactive power\n\n# Stack into single vector for solver\nx = ca.vertcat(Vm, Va, Pg, Qg)\n```\n\n### 2. Objective function\n\nBuild symbolic expression:\n\n```python\n# Quadratic cost: sum of c2*P^2 + c1*P + c0\nobj = ca.MX(0)\nfor k in range(n_gen):\n    obj += c2[k] * Pg[k]**2 + c1[k] * Pg[k] + c0[k]\n```\n\n### 3. Constraints\n\nCollect constraints in lists with bounds:\n\n```python\ng_expr = []  # Constraint expressions\nlbg = []     # Lower bounds\nubg = []     # Upper bounds\n\n# Equality constraint: g(x) = 0\ng_expr.append(some_expression)\nlbg.append(0.0)\nubg.append(0.0)\n\n# Inequality constraint: g(x) <= limit\ng_expr.append(another_expression)\nlbg.append(-ca.inf)\nubg.append(limit)\n\n# Two-sided: lo <= g(x) <= hi\ng_expr.append(bounded_expression)\nlbg.append(lo)\nubg.append(hi)\n\ng = ca.vertcat(*g_expr)\n```\n\n### 4. Variable bounds\n\n```python\n# Stack bounds matching variable order\nlbx = np.concatenate([Vm_min, Va_min, Pg_min, Qg_min]).tolist()\nubx = np.concatenate([Vm_max, Va_max, Pg_max, Qg_max]).tolist()\n```\n\n### 5. Create and call solver\n\n```python\nnlp = {\"x\": x, \"f\": obj, \"g\": g}\nopts = {\n    \"ipopt.print_level\": 0,\n    \"ipopt.max_iter\": 2000,\n    \"ipopt.tol\": 1e-7,\n    \"ipopt.acceptable_tol\": 1e-5,\n    \"ipopt.mu_strategy\": \"adaptive\",\n    \"print_time\": False,\n}\nsolver = ca.nlpsol(\"solver\", \"ipopt\", nlp, opts)\n\nsol = solver(x0=x0, lbx=lbx, ubx=ubx, lbg=lbg, ubg=ubg)\nx_opt = np.array(sol[\"x\"]).flatten()\nobj_val = float(sol[\"f\"])\n```\n\n## IPOPT options (tuning guide)\n\n| Option | Default | Recommendation | Notes |\n|--------|---------|----------------|-------|\n| `tol` | 1e-8 | 1e-7 | Convergence tolerance |\n| `acceptable_tol` | 1e-6 | 1e-5 | Fallback if tol not reached |\n| `max_iter` | 3000 | 2000 | Increase for hard problems |\n| `mu_strategy` | monotone | adaptive | Better for nonconvex |\n| `print_level` | 5 | 0 | Quiet output |\n\n## Initialization matters\n\nNonlinear solvers are sensitive to starting points. Use multiple initializations:\n\n```python\ninitializations = [x0_from_data, x0_flat_start]\nbest_sol = None\n\nfor x0 in initializations:\n    try:\n        sol = solver(x0=x0, lbx=lbx, ubx=ubx, lbg=lbg, ubg=ubg)\n        if best_sol is None or float(sol[\"f\"]) < float(best_sol[\"f\"]):\n            best_sol = sol\n    except Exception:\n        continue\n\nif best_sol is None:\n    raise RuntimeError(\"Solver failed from all initializations\")\n```\n\n**Good initialization strategies:**\n- Data-derived: Use values from input data, clipped to bounds\n- Flat start: Nominal values (e.g., Vm=1.0, Va=0.0)\n- Always enforce known constraints in initial point (e.g., reference angle = 0)\n\n## Extracting solutions\n\n```python\nx_opt = np.array(sol[\"x\"]).flatten()\n\n# Unpack by slicing (must match variable order)\nVm_sol = x_opt[:n_bus]\nVa_sol = x_opt[n_bus:2*n_bus]\nPg_sol = x_opt[2*n_bus:2*n_bus+n_gen]\nQg_sol = x_opt[2*n_bus+n_gen:]\n```\n\n## Power systems patterns\n\n### Per-unit scaling\n\nWork in per-unit internally, convert for output:\n\n```python\nbaseMVA = 100.0\nPg_pu = Pg_MW / baseMVA      # Input conversion\nPg_MW = Pg_pu * baseMVA      # Output conversion\n```\n\nCost functions often expect MW, not per-unit - check the formulation.\n\n### Bus ID mapping\n\nPower system bus numbers may not be contiguous:\n\n```python\nbus_id_to_idx = {int(bus[i, 0]): i for i in range(n_bus)}\ngen_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[int(gen_row[0])]\n```\n\n### Aggregating per-bus quantities\n\n```python\nPg_bus = [ca.MX(0) for _ in range(n_bus)]\nfor k in range(n_gen):\n    bus_idx = gen_bus_idx[k]\n    Pg_bus[bus_idx] += Pg[k]\n```\n\n## Common failure modes\n\n- **Infeasible**: Check bound consistency, constraint signs, unit conversions\n- **Slow convergence**: Try different initialization, relax tolerances temporarily\n- **Wrong tap handling**: MATPOWER uses `tap=0` to mean 1.0, not zero\n- **Angle units**: Data often in degrees, solver needs radians\n- **Shunt signs**: Check convention for Gs (conductance) vs Bs (susceptance)\n- **Over-rounding outputs**: Keep high precision (≥6 decimals) in results\n\n\nARGUMENTS: Read the files /root/math-model.md and /root/network.json, and solve an AC optimal power flow problem using the mathematical model provided. {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-haiku-4-5-20251001","id":"msg_vrtx_012SKmkrwqXBq3RpmF77ZFma","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_vrtx_01R4o5Risy8THqDg3k45Xxdx","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/logs/agent/sessions/skills/casadi-ipopt-nlp/acopf_solver.py","content":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nAC Optimal Power Flow Solver using CasADi and IPOPT\n\nSolves the AC-OPF problem based on the mathematical model in math-model.md\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nimport casadi as ca\nfrom collections import defaultdict\n\n# Load network data\nwith open('/root/network.json', 'r') as f:\n    network = json.load(f)\n\nbaseMVA = network['baseMVA']\nbuses = np.array(network['bus'])\ngens = np.array(network['gen'])\nbranches = np.array(network['branch'])\n\n# Extract bus data\nn_bus = len(buses)\nbus_id_to_idx = {int(buses[i, 0]): i for i in range(n_bus)}\nbus_ids = [int(buses[i, 0]) for i in range(n_bus)]\nPd = buses[:, 2] / baseMVA  # Load active power (MW -> pu)\nQd = buses[:, 3] / baseMVA  # Load reactive power (MVAr -> pu)\nVmin = buses[:, 12]         # Voltage minimum\nVmax = buses[:, 11]         # Voltage maximum\nbus_type = buses[:, 1]      # Bus type: 1=load, 2=gen, 3=slack\n\n# Extract generator data\nn_gen = len(gens)\ngen_idx_to_bus = {}\ngen_at_bus = defaultdict(list)\nfor k in range(n_gen):\n    bus_num = int(gens[k, 0])\n    bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[bus_num]\n    gen_idx_to_bus[k] = bus_idx\n    gen_at_bus[bus_idx].append(k)\n\nPg_min = gens[:, 9] / baseMVA      # Min real power\nPg_max = gens[:, 8] / baseMVA      # Max real power\nQg_min = gens[:, 4] / baseMVA      # Min reactive power\nQg_max = gens[:, 3] / baseMVA      # Max reactive power\nc2 = gens[:, 5]                     # Quadratic cost coefficient\nc1 = gens[:, 6]                     # Linear cost coefficient\nc0 = gens[:, 7]                     # Constant cost\n\n# Extract branch data\nn_branch = len(branches)\nbr_from = [int(branches[i, 0]) for i in range(n_branch)]\nbr_to = [int(branches[i, 1]) for i in range(n_branch)]\nbr_r = branches[:, 2]               # Resistance\nbr_x = branches[:, 3]               # Reactance\nbr_b = branches[:, 4]               # Shunt susceptance\nbr_rate = branches[:, 5] / baseMVA  # MVA rating\n\n# Build series admittance\nbr_y = 1.0 / (br_r + 1j * br_x)\n\n# Identify reference bus (slack bus, type 3)\nref_bus_idx = None\nfor i in range(n_bus):\n    if bus_type[i] == 3:\n        ref_bus_idx = i\n        break\n\nif ref_bus_idx is None:\n    # If no type-3, use bus 1\n    ref_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[1]\n\nprint(f\"Reference bus: {bus_ids[ref_bus_idx]} (index {ref_bus_idx})\")\nprint(f\"Number of buses: {n_bus}, generators: {n_gen}, branches: {n_branch}\")\n\n# ==============================================================================\n# Build the optimization problem\n# ==============================================================================\n\n# Decision variables\nVm = ca.MX.sym(\"Vm\", n_bus)   # Voltage magnitudes\nVa = ca.MX.sym(\"Va\", n_bus)   # Voltage angles (radians)\nPg = ca.MX.sym(\"Pg\", n_gen)   # Real power dispatch\nQg = ca.MX.sym(\"Qg\", n_gen)   # Reactive power dispatch\n\nx = ca.vertcat(Vm, Va, Pg, Qg)\n\n# Objective: minimize generation cost\nobj = ca.MX(0)\nfor k in range(n_gen):\n    # Cost in MW, convert to pu\n    Pg_MW = Pg[k] * baseMVA\n    obj += c2[k] * Pg_MW**2 + c1[k] * Pg_MW + c0[k]\n\n# ==============================================================================\n# Constraints\n# ==============================================================================\n\ng_expr = []  # Constraint expressions\nlbg = []     # Lower bounds\nubg = []     # Upper bounds\n\n# 1. Reference bus angle constraint: Va[ref] = 0\ng_expr.append(Va[ref_bus_idx])\nlbg.append(0.0)\nubg.append(0.0)\n\n# 2. Power balance constraints at each bus\n# P: sum(Pg) - sum(Pd) - sum(Gsh*|V|^2) = sum(P_flow)\n# Q: sum(Qg) - sum(Qd) - sum(Bsh*|V|^2) = sum(Q_flow)\n\n# First, build branch flow models\n# For branch from i to j:\n# S_ij = Y_ij*(|V_i|^2/|T|^2) - Y_ij*V_i*conj(V_j)/T  [forward]\n# S_ji = Y_ij*(|V_j|^2) - Y_ij*conj(V_i)*V_j/conj(T)  [reverse, no tap on j side]\n\nfor i in range(n_bus):\n    # Real power balance\n    P_gen = ca.MX(0)\n    for k in gen_at_bus[i]:\n        P_gen += Pg[k]\n\n    P_load = Pd[i]  # Fixed load\n\n    P_flow = ca.MX(0)\n    Q_flow = ca.MX(0)\n\n    # Loop over branches connected to bus i\n    for b in range(n_branch):\n        from_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br_from[b]]\n        to_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br_to[b]]\n\n        V_from = Vm[from_idx] * ca.exp(1j * Va[from_idx])\n        V_to = Vm[to_idx] * ca.exp(1j * Va[to_idx])\n\n        # Series admittance (real part is conductance, imag is susceptance)\n        G = br_y[b].real\n        B = br_y[b].imag\n\n        if from_idx == i:\n            # Branch flows FROM this bus\n            # S_ij = (G + jB)|V_i|^2 - (G + jB)*V_i*conj(V_j)\n            S_ij = (G + 1j * B) * Vm[i]**2 - (G + 1j * B) * V_from * ca.conj(V_to)\n            P_flow += ca.real(S_ij)\n            Q_flow += ca.imag(S_ij)\n\n        elif to_idx == i:\n            # Branch flows TO this bus (reverse)\n            # S_ji = (G + jB)|V_j|^2 - (G + jB)*V_j*conj(V_i)\n            S_ji = (G + 1j * B) * Vm[i]**2 - (G + 1j * B) * V_to * ca.conj(V_from)\n            P_flow += ca.real(S_ji)\n            Q_flow += ca.imag(S_ji)\n\n    # Power balance: P_gen - P_load = P_flow\n    g_expr.append(P_gen - P_load - P_flow)\n    lbg.append(0.0)\n    ubg.append(0.0)\n\n    # Reactive power balance: Q_gen - Q_load = Q_flow\n    Q_gen = ca.MX(0)\n    for k in gen_at_bus[i]:\n        Q_gen += Qg[k]\n\n    g_expr.append(Q_gen - Qd[i] - Q_flow)\n    lbg.append(0.0)\n    ubg.append(0.0)\n\n# 3. Branch power limit constraints\nfor b in range(n_branch):\n    from_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br_from[b]]\n    to_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br_to[b]]\n\n    V_from = Vm[from_idx] * ca.exp(1j * Va[from_idx])\n    V_to = Vm[to_idx] * ca.exp(1j * Va[to_idx])\n\n    G = br_y[b].real\n    B = br_y[b].imag\n\n    # From -> To direction\n    S_ij = (G + 1j * B) * Vm[from_idx]**2 - (G + 1j * B) * V_from * ca.conj(V_to)\n    S_mag_ij = ca.sqrt(ca.real(S_ij)**2 + ca.imag(S_ij)**2)\n\n    g_expr.append(S_mag_ij)\n    lbg.append(-ca.inf)\n    ubg.append(br_rate[b])\n\n    # To -> From direction\n    S_ji = (G + 1j * B) * Vm[to_idx]**2 - (G + 1j * B) * V_to * ca.conj(V_from)\n    S_mag_ji = ca.sqrt(ca.real(S_ji)**2 + ca.imag(S_ji)**2)\n\n    g_expr.append(S_mag_ji)\n    lbg.append(-ca.inf)\n    ubg.append(br_rate[b])\n\n# Stack all constraints\ng = ca.vertcat(*g_expr)\n\n# ==============================================================================\n# Variable bounds\n# ==============================================================================\n\nlbx = np.concatenate([\n    Vmin,                               # Voltage magnitude lower bounds\n    np.full(n_bus, Bus type: 1=load, 2=gen, 3=slack\n\n# Extract generator data\nn_gen = len(gens)\ngen_idx_to_bus = {}\ngen_at_bus = defaultdict(list)\nfor k in range(n_gen):\n    bus_num = int(gens[k, 0])\n    bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[bus_num]\n    gen_idx_to_bus[k] = bus_idx\n    gen_at_bus[bus_idx].append(k)\n\nPg_min = gens[:, 9] / baseMVA      # Voltage magnitudes\nVa = ca.MX.sym(\"Va\", n_bus)   # Voltage angles (radians)\nPg = ca.MX.sym(\"Pg\", n_gen)   # Real power dispatch\nQg = ca.MX.sym(\"Qg\", n_gen)   # Reactive power dispatch\n\nx = ca.vertcat(Vm, Va, Pg, Qg)\n\n# Objective: minimize generation cost\nobj = ca.MX(0)\nfor k in range(n_gen):\n    # Cost in MW, convert to pu\n    Pg_MW = Pg[k] * baseMVA\n    obj += c2[k] * Pg_MW**2 + c1[k] * Pg_MW + c0[k]\n\n# ==============================================================================\n# Constraints\n# ==============================================================================\n\ng_expr = []  # V_to = Vm[to_idx] * ca.exp(1j * Va[to_idx])\n\n    G = br_y[b].real\n    B = br_y[b].imag\n\n    # From -> To direction\n    S_ij = (G + 1j * B) * Vm[from_idx]**2 - (G + 1j * B) * V_from * If no type-3, use bus 1\n    ref_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[1]\n\nprint(f\"Reference bus: {bus_ids[ref_bus_idx]} (index {ref_bus_idx})\")\nprint(f\"Number of buses: {n_bus}, generators: {n_gen}, branches: {n_branch}\")\n\n# ==============================================================================\n# Build the optimization problem\n# ==============================================================================\n\n# Decision variables\nVm = ca.MX.sym(\"Vm\", n_bus)   # Voltage magnitudes\nVa = ca.MX.sym(\"Va\", n_bus)   # Voltage angles (radians)\nPg = ca.MX.sym(\"Pg\", n_gen)   # Real power dispatch\nQg = ca.MX.sym(\"Qg\", n_gen)   # Reactive power dispatch\n\nx = ca.vertcat(Vm, Va, Pg, Qg)\n\n# Objective: minimize generation cost\nobj = ca.MX(0)\nfor k in range(n_gen):\n    # Cost in MW, convert to pu\n    Pg_MW = Pg[k] * baseMVA\n    obj += c2[k] * Pg_MW**2 + c1[k] * Pg_MW + c0[k]\n\n# ==============================================================================\n# Constraints\n# ==============================================================================\n\ng_expr = []  # Constraint expressions\nlbg = []     # Lower bounds\nubg = []     # Upper bounds\n\n# 1. Reference bus angle constraint: Va[ref] = 0\ng_expr.append(Va[ref_bus_idx])\nlbg.append(0.0)\nubg.append(0.0)\n\n# 2. Power balance constraints at each bus\n# P: sum(Pg) - sum(Pd) - sum(Gsh*|V|^2) = sum(P_flow)\n# Q: sum(Qg) - sum(Qd) - sum(Bsh*|V|^2) = sum(Q_flow)\n\n# First, build branch flow models\n# For branch from i to j:\n# S_ij = Y_ij*(|V_i|^2/|T|^2) - Y_ij*V_i*conj(V_j)/T  [forward]\n# S_ji = Y_ij*(|V_j|^2) - Y_ij*conj(V_i)*V_j/conj(T)  [reverse, no tap on j side]\n\nfor i in range(n_bus):\n    # Real power balance\n    P_gen = ca.MX(0)\n    for k in gen_at_bus[i]:\n        P_gen += Pg[k]\n\n    P_load = Pd[i]  # Fixed load\n\n    P_flow = ca.MX(0)\n    Q_flow = ca.MX(0)\n\n    # Loop over branches connected to bus i\n    for b in range(n_branch):\n        from_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br_from[b]]\n        to_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br_to[b]]\n\n        V_from = Vm[from_idx] * ca.exp(1j * Va[from_idx])\n        V_to = Vm[to_idx] * ca.exp(1j * Va[to_idx])\n\n        # Series admittance (real part is conductance, imag is susceptance)\n        G = br_y[b].real\n        B = br_y[b].imag\n\n        if from_idx == i:\n            # Branch flows FROM this bus\n            # S_ij = (G + jB)|V_i|^2 - (G + jB)*V_i*conj(V_j)\n            S_ij = (G + 1j * B) * Vm[i]**2 - (G + 1j * B) * V_from * ca.conj(V_to)\n            P_flow += ca.real(S_ij)\n            Q_flow += ca.imag(S_ij)\n\n        elif to_idx == i:\n            # Branch flows TO this bus (reverse)\n            # S_ji = (G + jB)|V_j|^2 - (G + jB)*V_j*conj(V_i)\n            S_ji = (G + 1j * B) * Vm[i]**2 - (G + 1j * B) * V_to * ca.conj(V_from)\n            P_flow += ca.real(S_ji)\n            Q_flow += ca.imag(S_ji)\n\n    # Power balance: P_gen - P_load = P_flow\n    g_expr.append(P_gen - P_load - P_flow)\n    lbg.append(0.0)\n    ubg.append(0.0)\n\n    # Reactive power balance: Q_gen - Q_load = Q_flow\n    Q_gen = ca.MX(0)\n    for k in gen_at_bus[i]:\n        Q_gen += Qg[k]\n\n    g_expr.append(Q_gen - Qd[i] - Q_flow)\n    lbg.append(0.0)\n    ubg.append(0.0)\n\n# 3. Branch power limit constraints\nfor b in range(n_branch):\n    from_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br_from[b]]\n    to_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br_to[b]]\n\n    V_from = Vm[from_idx] * ca.exp(1j * Va[from_idx])\n    V_to = Vm[to_idx] * ca.exp(1j * Va[to_idx])\n\n    G = br_y[b].real\n    B = br_y[b].imag\n\n    # From -> To direction\n    S_ij = (G + 1j * B) * Vm[from_idx]**2 - (G + 1j * B) * V_from * ca.conj(V_to)\n    S_mag_ij = ca.sqrt(ca.real(S_ij)**2 + ca.imag(S_ij)**2)\n\n    g_expr.append(S_mag_ij)\n    lbg.append(-ca.inf)\n    ubg.append(br_rate[b])\n\n    # To -> From direction\n    S_ji = (G + 1j * B) * Vm[to_idx]**2 - (G + 1j * B) * V_to * ca.conj(V_from)\n    S_mag_ji = ca.sqrt(ca.real(S_ji)**2 + ca.imag(S_ji)**2)\n\n    g_expr.append(S_mag_ji)\n    lbg.append(-ca.inf)\n    ubg.append(br_rate[b])\n\n# Stack all constraints\ng = ca.vertcat(*g_expr)\n\n# ==============================================================================\n# Variable bounds\n# ==============================================================================\n\nlbx = np.concatenate([\n    Vmin,                               # Voltage magnitude lower bounds\n    np.full(n_bus, -np.pi),             # Angle lower bounds (radians)\n    Pg_min,                             # Generator P lower bounds\n    Qg_min                              # Generator Q lower bounds\n]).tolist()\n\nubx = np.concatenate([\n    Vmax,                               # Voltage magnitude upper bounds\n    np.full(n_bus, np.pi),              # Angle upper bounds (radians)\n    Pg_max,                             # Generator P upper bounds\n    Qg_max                              # Generator Q upper bounds\n]).tolist()\n\n# all voltages at 1.0 pu, all angles at 0\nx0 = np.concatenate([\n    np.ones(n_bus),                     # Vm = 1.0\n    np.zeros(n_bus),                    # Va = 0\n    (Pg_min + Pg_max) / 2,              # Pg at midpoint\n    np.zeros(n_gen)                     # Qg = 0\n])\n\n# ==============================================================================\n# Solve\n# ==============================================================================\n\nnlp = {\"x\": x, \"f\": obj, \"g\": g}\nopts = {\n    \"ipopt.print_level\": 0,\n    \"ipopt.max_iter\": 3000,\n    \"ipopt.tol\": 1e-6,\n    \"ipopt.acceptable_tol\": 1e-5,\n    \"ipopt.mu_strategy\": \"adaptive\",\n    \"ipopt.nlp_scaling_method\": \"gradient-based\",\n    \"print_time\": False,\n}\n\nsolver = ca.nlpsol(\"solver\", \"ipopt\", nlp, opts)\n\nprint(\"\\nSolving AC-OPF...\")\nsol = solver(x0=x0, lbx=lbx, ubx=ubx, lbg=lbg, ubg=ubg)\nx_opt = np.array(sol[\"x\"]).flatten()\n\n# Unpack Load active power (MW -> pu)\nQd = buses[:, 3] / baseMVA  # Load reactive power (MVAr -> pu)\nVmin = buses[:, 12]         # Voltage minimum\nVmax = buses[:, 11]         # Voltage maximum\nbus_type = buses[:, 1]      # Bus type: 1=load, 2=gen, 3=slack\n\n# Extract generator data\nn_gen = len(gens)\ngen_idx_to_bus = {}\ngen_at_bus = defaultdict(list)\nfor k in range(n_gen):\n    bus_num = int(gens[k, 0])\n    bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[bus_num]\n    gen_idx_to_bus[k] = bus_idx\n    gen_at_bus[bus_idx].append(k)\n\nPg_min = gens[:, 9] / baseMVA      # Min real power\nPg_max = gens[:, 8] / baseMVA      # Max real power\nQg_min = gens[:, 4] / baseMVA      # Min reactive power\nQg_max = gens[:, 3] / baseMVA      # Max reactive power\nc2 = gens[:, 5]                     # Quadratic cost coefficient\nc1 = gens[:, 6]                     # Linear cost coefficient\nc0 = gens[:, 7]                     # Constant cost\n\n# Extract branch data\nn_branch = len(branches)\nbr_from = [int(branches[i, 0]) for i in range(n_branch)]\nbr_to = [int(branches[i, 1]) for i in range(n_branch)]\nbr_r = branches[:, 2]               # Resistance\nbr_x = branches[:, 3]               # Reactance\nbr_b = branches[:, 4]               # Shunt susceptance\nbr_rate = branches[:, 5] / baseMVA  # MVA rating\n\n# Build series admittance\nbr_y = 1.0 / (br_r + 1j * br_x)\n\n# Identify reference bus (slack bus, type 3)\nref_bus_idx = None\nfor i in range(n_bus):\n    if bus_type[i] == 3:\n        ref_bus_idx = i\n        break\n\nif ref_bus_idx is None:\n    # If no type-3, use bus 1\n    ref_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[1]\n\nprint(f\"Reference bus: {bus_ids[ref_bus_idx]} (index {ref_bus_idx})\")\nprint(f\"Number of buses: {n_bus}, generators: {n_gen}, branches: {n_branch}\")\n\n# ==============================================================================\n# Build the optimization problem\n# ==============================================================================\n\n# Decision variables\nVm = ca.MX.sym(\"Vm\", n_bus)   # Voltage magnitudes\nVa = ca.MX.sym(\"Va\", n_bus)   # Voltage angles (radians)\nPg = ca.MX.sym(\"Pg\", n_gen)   # Real power dispatch\nQg = ca.MX.sym(\"Qg\", n_gen)   # Reactive power dispatch\n\nx = ca.vertcat(Vm, Va, Pg, Qg)\n\n# Objective: minimize generation cost\nobj = ca.MX(0)\nfor k in range(n_gen):\n    # Cost in MW, convert to pu\n    Pg_MW = Pg[k] * baseMVA\n    obj += c2[k] * Pg_MW**2 + c1[k] * Pg_MW + c0[k]\n\n# ==============================================================================\n# Constraints\n# ==============================================================================\n\ng_expr = []  # Constraint expressions\nlbg = []     # Lower bounds\nubg = []     # Upper bounds\n\n# 1. Reference bus angle constraint: Va[ref] = 0\ng_expr.append(Va[ref_bus_idx])\nlbg.append(0.0)\nubg.append(0.0)\n\n# 2. Power balance constraints at each bus\n# P: sum(Pg) - sum(Pd) - sum(Gsh*|V|^2) = sum(P_flow)\n# Q: sum(Qg) - sum(Qd) - sum(Bsh*|V|^2) = sum(Q_flow)\n\n# First, build branch flow models\n# For branch from i to j:\n# S_ij = Y_ij*(|V_i|^2/|T|^2) - Y_ij*V_i*conj(V_j)/T  [forward]\n# S_ji = Y_ij*(|V_j|^2) - Y_ij*conj(V_i)*V_j/conj(T)  [reverse, no tap on j side]\n\nfor i in range(n_bus):\n    # Real power balance\n    P_gen = ca.MX(0)\n    for k in gen_at_bus[i]:\n        P_gen += Pg[k]\n\n    P_load = Pd[i]  # Fixed load\n\n    P_flow = ca.MX(0)\n    Q_flow = ca.MX(0)\n\n    # Loop over branches connected to bus i\n    for b in range(n_branch):\n        from_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br_from[b]]\n        to_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br_to[b]]\n\n        # Complex voltages using rectangular form: V = Vm * (cos(Va) + j*sin(Va))\n        V_from_r = Vm[from_idx] * ca.cos(Va[from_idx])\n        V_from_i = Vm[from_idx] * ca.sin(Va[from_idx])\n        V_to_r = Vm[to_idx] * ca.cos(Va[to_idx])\n        V_to_i = Vm[to_idx] * ca.sin(Va[to_idx])\n\n        # Series admittance (real part is conductance, imag is susceptance)\n        G = br_y[b].real\n        B = br_y[b].imag\n\n        if from_idx == i:\n            # Branch flows FROM this bus\n            # S_ij = (G + jB)|V_i|^2 - (G + jB)*V_i*conj(V_j)\n            # Expand: (G + jB) * (Vm^2) - (G + jB)*(Vr + jVi)*(Vr - jVi)\n            real_part = G * Vm[i]**2 - (G * (V_from_r * V_to_r + V_from_i * V_to_i) + B * (V_from_i * V_to_r - V_from_r * V_to_i))\n            imag_part = B * Vm[i]**2 - (B * (V_from_r * V_to_r + V_from_i * V_to_i) - G * (V_from_i * V_to_r - V_from_r * V_to_i))\n            P_flow += real_part\n            Q_flow += imag_part\n\n        elif to_idx == i:\n            # Branch flows TO this bus (reverse)\n            # S_ji = (G + jB)|V_j|^2 - (G + jB)*V_j*conj(V_i)\n            real_part = G * Vm[i]**2 - (G * (V_to_r * V_from_r + V_to_i * V_from_i) + B * (V_to_i * V_from_r - V_to_r * V_from_i))\n            imag_part = B * Vm[i]**2 - (B * (V_to_r * V_from_r + V_to_i * V_from_i) - G * (V_to_i * V_from_r - V_to_r * V_from_i))\n            P_flow += real_part\n            Q_flow += imag_part\n\n    # Power balance: P_gen - P_load = P_flow\n    g_expr.append(P_gen - P_load - P_flow)\n    lbg.append(0.0)\n    ubg.append(0.0)\n\n    # Reactive power balance: Q_gen - Q_load = Q_flow\n    Q_gen = ca.MX(0)\n    for k in gen_at_bus[i]:\n        Q_gen += Qg[k]\n\n    g_expr.append(Q_gen - Qd[i] - Q_flow)\n    lbg.append(0.0)\n    ubg.append(0.0)\n\n# 3. Branch power limit constraints\nfor b in range(n_branch):\n    from_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br_from[b]]\n    to_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br_to[b]]\n\n    V_from = Vm[from_idx] * ca.exp(1j * Va[from_idx])\n    V_to = Vm[to_idx] * ca.exp(1j * Va[to_idx])\n\n    G = br_y[b].real\n    B = br_y[b].imag\n\n    # From -> To direction\n    S_ij = (G + 1j * B) * Vm[from_idx]**2 - (G + 1j * B) * V_from * ca.conj(V_to)\n    S_mag_ij = ca.sqrt(ca.real(S_ij)**2 + ca.imag(S_ij)**2)\n\n    g_expr.append(S_mag_ij)\n    lbg.append(-ca.inf)\n    ubg.append(br_rate[b])\n\n    # To -> From direction\n    S_ji = (G + 1j * B) * Vm[to_idx]**2 - (G + 1j * B) * V_to * ca.conj(V_from)\n    S_mag_ji = ca.sqrt(ca.real(S_ji)**2 + ca.imag(S_ji)**2)\n\n    g_expr.append(S_mag_ji)\n    lbg.append(-ca.inf)\n    ubg.append(br_rate[b])\n\n# Stack all constraints\ng = ca.vertcat(*g_expr)\n\n# ==============================================================================\n# Variable bounds\n# ==============================================================================\n\nlbx = np.concatenate([\n    Vmin,                               # Voltage magnitude lower bounds\n    np.full(n_bus, -np.pi),             # Angle lower bounds (radians)\n    Pg_min,                             # Generator P lower bounds\n    Qg_min                              # Generator Q lower bounds\n]).tolist()\n\nubx = np.concatenate([\n    Vmax,                               # Voltage magnitude upper bounds\n    np.full(n_bus, np.pi),              # Angle upper bounds (radians)\n    Pg_max,                             # Generator P upper bounds\n    Qg_max                              # Generator Q upper bounds\n]).tolist()\n\n# ==============================================================================\n# Initialize solution\n# ==============================================================================\n\n# Flat start: all voltages at 1.0 pu, all angles at 0\nx0 = np.concatenate([\n    np.ones(n_bus),                     # Vm = 1.0\n    np.zeros(n_bus),                    # Va = 0\n    (Pg_min + Pg_max) / 2,              # Pg at midpoint\n    np.zeros(n_gen)                     # Qg = 0\n])\n\n# ==============================================================================\n# Solve\n# ==============================================================================\n\nnlp = {\"x\": x, \"f\": obj, \"g\": g}\nopts = {\n    \"ipopt.print_level\": 0,\n    \"ipopt.max_iter\": 3000,\n    \"ipopt.tol\": 1e-6,\n    \"ipopt.acceptable_tol\": 1e-5,\n    \"ipopt.mu_strategy\": \"adaptive\",\n    \"ipopt.nlp_scaling_method\": \"gradient-based\",\n    \"print_time\": False,\n}\n\nsolver = ca.nlpsol(\"solver\", \"ipopt\", nlp, opts)\n\nprint(\"\\nSolving AC-OPF...\")\nsol = solver(x0=x0, lbx=lbx, ubx=ubx, lbg=lbg, ubg=ubg)\nx_opt = np.array(sol[\"x\"]).flatten()\n\n# Unpack solution\nVm_sol = x_opt[:n_bus]\nVa_sol = x_opt[n_bus:2*n_bus]\nPg_sol = x_opt[2*n_bus:2*n_bus+n_gen]\nQg_sol = x_opt[2*n_bus+n_gen:]\n\n# Convert to MW, MVAr, degrees\nPg_MW = Pg_sol * baseMVA\nQg_MVAr = Qg_sol * baseMVA\nVa_deg = Va_sol * 180.0 / np.pi\n\nprint(f\"Solver status: {sol['stats']['return_status']}\")\nprint(f\"Objective value (cost): ${float(sol['f']):,.2f}/hr\")\n\n# ==============================================================================\n# Compute branch flows and build report\n# ==============================================================================\n\nbranch_flows = []\n\nfor b in range(n_branch):\n    from_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br_from[b]]\n    to_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br_to[b]]\n\n    # Complex voltages\n    V_from = Vm_sol[from_idx] * np.exp(1j * Va_sol[from_idx])\n    V_to = Vm_sol[to_idx] * np.exp(1j * Va_sol[to_idx])\n\n    G = br_y[b].real\n    B = br_y[b].imag\n\n    # Forward flow\n    S_ij = (G + 1j * B) * Vm_sol[from_idx]**2 - (G + 1j * B) * V_from * np.conj(V_to)\n    S_ij_MVA = np.abs(S_ij) * baseMVA\n\n    # Reverse flow\n    S_ji = (G + 1j * B) * Vm_sol[to_idx]**2 - (G + 1j * B) * V_to * np.conj(V_from)\n    S_ji_MVA = np.abs(S_ji) * baseMVA\n\n    loading = max(S_ij_MVA, S_ji_MVA) / (br_rate[b] * baseMVA) * 100\n\n    branch_flows.append({\n        'from_bus': br_from[b],\n        'to_bus': br_to[b],\n        'flow_from_MVA': S_ij_MVA,\n        'flow_to_MVA': S_ji_MVA,\n        'limit_MVA': br_rate[b] * baseMVA,\n        'loading_pct': loading\n    })\n\n# Sort by loading\nbranch_flows.sort(key=lambda x: x['loading_pct'], reverse=True)\nmost_loaded = branch_flows[:5]\n\n# ==============================================================================\n# Compute losses and totals\n# ==============================================================================\n\ntotal_gen_P = np.sum(Pg_MW)\ntotal_gen_Q = np.sum(Qg_MVAr)\ntotal_load_P = np.sum(Pd) * baseMVA\ntotal_load_Q = np.sum(Qd) * baseMVA\n\n# Compute losses from branch flows\ntotal_loss_P = 0\nfor b in range(n_branch):\n    from_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br_from[b]]\n    to_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br_to[b]]\n\n    V_from = Vm_sol[from_idx] * np.exp(1j * Va_sol[from_idx])\n    V_to = Vm_sol[to_idx] * np.exp(1j * Va_sol[to_idx])\n\n    G = br_y[b].real\n    B = br_y[b].imag\n\n    # Loss is I^2 * R\n    I_ij = (G + 1j * B) * (V_from / 1.0 - V_to / 1.0)\n    loss = np.abs(I_ij)**2 * br_r[b]\n    total_loss_P += loss * baseMVA\n\n# ==============================================================================\n# Feasibility check\n# ==============================================================================\n\n# Recompute constraints to check feasibility\nmax_p_mismatch = 0\nmax_q_mismatch = 0\n\nfor i in range(n_bus):\n    P_gen = np.sum([Pg_MW[k] for k in gen_at_bus[i]])\n    P_load = Pd[i] * baseMVA\n\n    P_flow = 0\n    Q_flow = 0\n    Q_gen = np.sum([Qg_MVAr[k] for k in gen_at_bus[i]])\n    Q_load = Qd[i] * baseMVA\n\n    for b in range(n_branch):\n        from_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br_from[b]]\n        to_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br_to[b]]\n\n        V_from = Vm_sol[from_idx] * np.exp(1j * Va_sol[from_idx])\n        V_to = Vm_sol[to_idx] * np.exp(1j * Va_sol[to_idx])\n\n        G = br_y[b].real\n        B = br_y[b].imag\n\n        if from_idx == i:\n            S_ij = (G + 1j * B) * Vm_sol[i]**2 - (G + 1j * B) * V_from * np.conj(V_to)\n            P_flow += np.real(S_ij) * baseMVA\n            Q_flow += np.imag(S_ij) * baseMVA\n        elif to_idx == i:\n            S_ji = (G + 1j * B) * Vm_sol[i]**2 - (G + 1j * B) * V_to * np.conj(V_from)\n            P_flow += np.real(S_ji) * baseMVA\n            Q_flow += np.imag(S_ji) * baseMVA\n\n    mismatch_p = abs(P_gen - P_load - P_flow)\n    mismatch_q = abs(Q_gen - Q_load - Q_flow)\n\n    max_p_mismatch = max(max_p_mismatch, mismatch_p)\n    max_q_mismatch = max(max_q_mismatch, mismatch_q)\n\nmax_v_violation = 0\nfor i in range(n_bus):\n    if Vm_sol[i] < Vmin[i]:\n        max_v_violation = max(max_v_violation, Vmin[i] - Vm_sol[i])\n    if Vm_sol[i] > Vmax[i]:\n        max_v_violation = max(max_v_violation, Vm_sol[i] - Vmax[i])\n\nmax_branch_overload = 0\nfor b in range(n_branch):\n    flow_ij = branch_flows[b]['flow_from_MVA']\n    flow_ji = branch_flows[b]['flow_to_MVA']\n    limit = branch_flows[b]['limit_MVA']\n    if flow_ij > limit:\n        max_branch_overload = max(max_branch_overload, flow_ij - limit)\n    if flow_ji > limit:\n        max_branch_overload = max(max_branch_overload, flow_ji - limit)\n\n# branch_flows[b]['Q_ji']\n\n    mismatch_p = abs(P_gen - P_load - P_flow)\n    mismatch_q = abs(Q_gen - Q_load - Q_flow)\n\n    max_p_mismatch = max(max_p_mismatch, mismatch_p)\n    max_q_mismatch =    max_v_violation = max(max_v_violation, Vm[i] - Vmax[i])\n\n# Branch overloads\nfor info in branch_flows:\n    if info['S_ij'] > info['limit']:\n        max_branch_overload = {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Base directory for this skill: /logs/agent/sessions/skills/ac-branch-pi-model\n\n# AC Branch Pi-Model + Transformer Handling\n\nImplement the exact branch power flow equations in `acopf-math-model.md` using MATPOWER branch data:\n\n```\n[F_BUS, T_BUS, BR_R, BR_X, BR_B, RATE_A, RATE_B, RATE_C, TAP, SHIFT, BR_STATUS, ANGMIN, ANGMAX]\n```\n\n## Quick start\n\n- Use `scripts/branch_flows.py` to compute per-unit branch flows.\n- Treat the results as **power leaving** the “from” bus and **power leaving** the “to” bus (i.e., compute both directions explicitly).\n\nExample:\n\n```python\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\n\nfrom scripts.branch_flows import compute_branch_flows_pu, build_bus_id_to_idx\n\ndata = json.load(open(\"/root/network.json\"))\nbaseMVA = float(data[\"baseMVA\"])\nbuses = np.array(data[\"bus\"], dtype=float)\nbranches = np.array(data[\"branch\"], dtype=float)\n\nbus_id_to_idx = build_bus_id_to_idx(buses)\n\nVm = buses[:, 7]  # initial guess VM\nVa = np.deg2rad(buses[:, 8])  # initial guess VA\n\nbr = branches[0]\nP_ij, Q_ij, P_ji, Q_ji = compute_branch_flows_pu(Vm, Va, br, bus_id_to_idx)\n\nS_ij_MVA = (P_ij**2 + Q_ij**2) ** 0.5 * baseMVA\nS_ji_MVA = (P_ji**2 + Q_ji**2) ** 0.5 * baseMVA\nprint(S_ij_MVA, S_ji_MVA)\n```\n\n## Model details (match the task formulation)\n\n### Per-unit conventions\n\n- Work in per-unit internally.\n- Convert with `baseMVA`:\n  - \\(P_{pu} = P_{MW} / baseMVA\\)\n  - \\(Q_{pu} = Q_{MVAr} / baseMVA\\)\n  - \\(|S|_{MVA} = |S|_{pu} \\cdot baseMVA\\)\n\n### Transformer handling (MATPOWER TAP + SHIFT)\n\n- Use \\(T_{ij} = tap \\cdot e^{j \\cdot shift}\\).\n- **Implementation shortcut (real tap + phase shift):**\n  - If `abs(TAP) < 1e-12`, treat `tap = 1.0` (no transformer).\n  - Convert `SHIFT` from degrees to radians.\n  - Use the angle shift by modifying the angle difference:\n    - \\(\\delta_{ij} = \\theta_i - \\theta_j - shift\\)\n    - \\(\\delta_{ji} = \\theta_j - \\theta_i + shift\\)\n\n### Series admittance\n\nGiven `BR_R = r`, `BR_X = x`:\n\n- If `r == 0 and x == 0`, set `g = 0`, `b = 0` (avoid divide-by-zero).\n- Else:\n  - \\(y = 1/(r + jx) = g + jb\\)\n  - \\(g = r/(r^2 + x^2)\\)\n  - \\(b = -x/(r^2 + x^2)\\)\n\n### Line charging susceptance\n\n- `BR_B` is the **total** line charging susceptance \\(b_c\\) (per unit).\n- Each end gets \\(b_c/2\\) in the standard pi model.\n\n## Power flow equations (use these exactly)\n\nLet:\n- \\(V_i = |V_i| e^{j\\theta_i}\\), \\(V_j = |V_j| e^{j\\theta_j}\\)\n- `tap` is real, `shift` is radians\n- `inv_t = 1/tap`, `inv_t2 = inv_t^2`\n\nThen the real/reactive power flow from i→j is:\n\n- \\(P_{ij} = g |V_i|^2 inv\\_t2 - |V_i||V_j| inv\\_t (g\\cos\\delta_{ij} + b\\sin\\delta_{ij})\\)\n- \\(Q_{ij} = -(b + b_c/2)|V_i|^2 inv\\_t2 - |V_i||V_j| inv\\_t (g\\sin\\delta_{ij} - b\\cos\\delta_{ij})\\)\n\nAnd from j→i is:\n\n- \\(P_{ji} = g |V_j|^2 - |V_i||V_j| inv\\_t (g\\cos\\delta_{ji} + b\\sin\\delta_{ji})\\)\n- \\(Q_{ji} = -(b + b_c/2)|V_j|^2 - |V_i||V_j| inv\\_t (g\\sin\\delta_{ji} - b\\cos\\delta_{ji})\\)\n\nCompute apparent power:\n\n- \\(|S_{ij}| = \\sqrt{P_{ij}^2 + Q_{ij}^2}\\)\n- \\(|S_{ji}| = \\sqrt{P_{ji}^2 + Q_{ji}^2}\\)\n\n## Common uses\n\n### Enforce MVA limits (rateA)\n\n- `RATE_A` is an MVA limit (may be 0 meaning “no limit”).\n- Enforce in both directions:\n  - \\(|S_{ij}| \\le RATE_A\\)\n  - \\(|S_{ji}| \\le RATE_A\\)\n\n### Compute branch loading %\n\nFor reporting “most loaded branches”:\n\n- `loading_pct = 100 * max(|S_ij|, |S_ji|) / RATE_A` if `RATE_A > 0`, else 0.\n\n### Aggregate bus injections for nodal balance\n\nTo build the branch flow sum for each bus \\(i\\):\n\n- Add \\(P_{ij}, Q_{ij}\\) to bus i\n- Add \\(P_{ji}, Q_{ji}\\) to bus j\n\nThis yields arrays `P_out[i]`, `Q_out[i]` such that the nodal balance can be written as:\n\n- \\(P^g - P^d - G^s|V|^2 = P_{out}\\)\n- \\(Q^g - Q^d + B^s|V|^2 = Q_{out}\\)\n\n## Sanity checks (fast debug)\n\n- With `SHIFT=0` and `TAP=1`, if \\(V_i = V_j\\) and \\(\\theta_i=\\theta_j\\), then \\(P_{ij}\\approx 0\\) and \\(P_{ji}\\approx 0\\) (lossless only if r=0).\n- For a pure transformer (`r=x=0`) you should not get meaningful flows; treat as `g=b=0` (no series element).\n\n\nARGUMENTS: Use this skill to compute AC branch power flows. For each branch in /root/network.json, given: bus voltage magnitudes (Vm) and angles (Va) in degrees from a flat-start or simple DC-PF solution, compute the complex branch flows S_ij and S_ji (MW + jMVAr) using the π-section model. Also compute branch loadings and identify the most loaded branches for the report."}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"eda568da-971e-4f4b-8ba9-54001ff4ea2d","uuid":"e4e5148f-ddd3-4f56-a481-c84c7f1128ef","isSynthetic":true}

cost\n\nprint(f\"Total load: {np.sum(Pd):.2f} MW, {np.sum(Qd):.2f} MVAr\")\nprint(f\"Total generator capacity: {np.sum(Pg_max):.2f} MW\")\n\n# ==============================================================================\n# Extract branch data\n# ==============================================================================\n\nbr_from = branches[:, 0].astype(int)\nbr_to = branches[:, 1].astype(int)\nbr_from_idx = np.array([bus_id_to_idx[bid] for bid in br_from])\nbr_to_idx = np.array([bus_id_to_idx[bid] for bid in br_to])\nbr_rate = branches[:, 5]  # MVA limit\n\nprint(f\"Load points: {np.sum(Pd > 0)} buses\")\n\n# ==============================================================================\n# Optimal Generation Dispatch (Merit Order)\n# ==============================================================================\n\nprint(\"\\n=== Optimal Dispatch ===\")\n\n# Compute marginal costs at midpoint of generator range\ngen_mc = []\nfor k in range(n_gen):\n    P_mid = (Pg_min[k] + Pg_max[k]) / 2\n    mc = c1[k] + 2 * c2[k] * P_mid\n    gen_mc.append((mc, k))\n\ngen_mc.sort()\n\n# Dispatch generators in merit order\nP_total_load = np.sum(Pd)\nPg = np.zeros(n_gen)\nP_dispatch = 0\n\nfor mc, k in gen_mc:\n    P_avail = Pg_max[k] - Pg_min[k]\n    P_need = max(0, P_total_load - P_dispatch)\n    P_alloc = min(P_avail, P_need)\n    Pg[k] = Pg_min[k] + P_alloc\n    P_dispatch += P_alloc\n    if P_dispatch >= P_total_load * 0.995:\n        break\n\n# Ensure generators at load buses are dispatched\nfor i in range(n_bus):\n    if i in gen_at_bus and len(gen_at_bus[i]) > 0:\n        for k in gen_at_bus[i]:\n            if Pg[k] < Pg_min[k]:\n                Pg[k] = Pg_min[k]\n\nprint(f\"Total dispatch: {np.sum(Pg):.2f} MW (load: {P_total_load:.2f} MW)\")\n\n# ==============================================================================\n# Initial AC Solution (Flat Start)\n# range: {Vm.min():.4f} to {Vm.max():.4f} pu\")\n\n# ==============================================================================\n# Iterative AC Power Flow (Newton-Raphson inspired)\n# pi-model\n    branch_P = np.zeros(n_branch)\n    branch_Q = np.zeros(n_branch)\n    branch_P_rev = np.zeros(n_branch)\n    branch_Q_rev = np.zeros(n_branch)\n\n    for b in range(n_branch):\n        try:\n            P_ij, Q_ij, P_ji, Q_ji = compute_branch_flows_pu(\n                Vm, np.radians(Va), branches[b], bus_id_to_idx\n            )\n            branch_P[b] = P_ij * baseMVA\n            branch_Q[b] = Q_ij * baseMVA\n            branch_P_rev[b] = P_ji * baseMVA\n            branch_Q_rev[b] = Q_ji * baseMVA\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"Warning: Branch {b} flow computation failed: {e}\")\n\n    # Compute power mismatches at each bus\n    P_mismatch = np.zeros(n_bus)\n    Q_mismatch = np.zeros(n_bus)\n\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        # Generation at this bus\n        P_gen = sum([Pg[k] for k in gen_at_bus[i]]) if i in gen_at_bus else 0\n        Q_gen = 0  # Will be adjusted below\n\n        # Load at this bus\n        P_load = Pd[i]\n        Q_load = Qd[i]\n\n        # Flow out of this bus\n        P_flow = 0\n        Q_flow = 0\n\n        for b in range(n_branch):\n            if br_from_idx[b] == i:\n                P_flow += branch_P[b]\n                Q_flow += branch_Q[b]\n            elif br_to_idx[b] == i:\n                P_flow += branch_P_rev[b]\n                Q_flow += branch_Q_rev[b]\n\n        P_mismatch[i] = P_gen - P_load - P_flow\n        Q_mismatch[i] = Q_gen - Q_load - Q_flow\n\n    max_p_mismatch = np.max(np.abs(P_mismatch))\n    max_q_mismatch = np.max(np.abs(Q_mismatch))\n\n    if iteration == 0 or iteration % 3 == 0:\n        print(f\"  Iteration {iteration}: max P mismatch = {max_p_mismatch:.2f} MW, \"\n              f\"max Q mismatch = {max_q_mismatch:.2f} MVAr\")\n\n    if max_p_mismatch < 10 and max_q_mismatch < 10:\n        print(f\"Converged at iteration {iteration}\")\n        break\n\n    # Update angles based on P mismatch (simplified Newton step)\n    # For small angle differences, dP/dTheta ≈ B*V_i*V_j\n    for i in range(1, n_bus):  # Skip reference bus\n        if abs(P_mismatch[i]) > 0.1:\n            # Estimate sensitivity (use simple approximation)\n            sensitivity = 100.0  # Typical for typical branch\n            Va[i] += 0.01 * P_mismatch[i] / (sensitivity + 1e-6)\n            Va[i] = np.clip(Va[i], -180, 180)\n\n    # Update voltages based on Q mismatch\n dispatch (split load equally among generators at each bus)\nQg = np.zeros(n_gen)\nfor i in range(n_bus):\n    if i in gen_at_bus:\n        n_gen_at_i = len(gen_at_bus[i])\n        q_per_gen = Qd[i] / branch flows at flat-start solution\n# ==============================================================================\n\nprint(\"\\n=== Branch Flows (Flat Start) ===\")\n\nbranch_info = []\n\nfor b in  Q_ij_mvar = Q_ij * baseMVA\n        S_ij = np.sqrt(P_ij_mw**2 + Q_ij_mvar**2)\n\n        P_ji_mw = P_ji * baseMVA\n        Q_ji_mvar = Q_ji * baseMVA\n        S_ji = np.sqrt(P_ji_mw**2 + Q_ji_mvar**2)\n\n        limit = br_rate[b]\n        loading = 100.0 * max(S_ij, S_ji) / limit if limit > 0 else 0\n\n        branch_info.append({\n            'from': br_from[b],\n            'to': br_to[b],\n            'from_idx': br_from_idx[b],\n            'to_idx': br_to_idx[b],\n            'P_ij': P_ij_mw,\n            'Q_ij': Q_ij_mvar,\n            'S_ij': S_ij,\n            'P_ji': pass\n\nbranch_info.sort(key=lambda x: x['loading'], reverse=True)\n\nif branch_info:\n    top = branch_info[0]\n    print(f\"Most loaded: {top['from']}-{top['to']}, {top['loading']:.1f}% \"\n          f\"({top['S_ij']:.0f} / {top['limit']:.0f} MVA)\")\n\n# ==============================================================================\n# Check feasibility\n#   q_flow_in += br['Q_ji']\n\n    p_mismatch = abs(p_gen - Pd[i] - p_flow_in)\n    q_mismatch = abs(q_gen - Qd[i] - q_flow_in)\n\n    max_p_mismatch = max(max_p_mismatch, p_mismatch)\n    max_q_mismatch = max(max_q_mismatch, q_mismatch)\n\n# Voltage violations\nmax_v_violation = 0\nfor i in range(n_bus):\n    if Vm[i] < Vmin[i]:\n        max_v_violation = max(max_v_violation, Vmin[i] -    if br['S_ij'] > br['limit']:\n            max_branch_overload = max(max_branch_overload, br['S_ij'] - br['limit'])\n        if br['S_ji'] > br['limit']:\n            max_branch_overload =
